# 86ac5b9 Merge pull request #9 from steven91007/gitkb

## Summary
Merges PR #9 (branch gitkb) into master: the Langfuse SDK upgrade to 4.15.6 with offline tests for the tracing module, the matching .env.example and lockfile changes, and the refreshed gitkb notes.

## Why
Lands the Langfuse v4 readiness work and the knowledge-base update reviewed in PR #9.

## Risks
Langfuse v4 changes SDK behaviour; tracing setup should be checked in production. Otherwise none beyond the merged commits.
